Source-to-sink taint tracing, no-server-roundtrip exploitation, and framework-specific sinks. Tier 1. Payloads: payloads/xss.md + client-side.md. ([AnandJogawade/Zazy-Pentest-Plugin-for-Claude](https://github.com/AnandJogawade/Zazy-Pentest-Plugin-for-Claude/tree/HEAD/zazy-pentest/skills/dom-xss) · ★28)
- **dom-xss**: DOM-based XSS occurs when JavaScript reads attacker-controlled sources (`location.hash`, `document.referrer`, `window.name`, `location.search`) and passes them to dangerous sinks (`document.write`, `innerHTML`, `eval`, `location.href`, `setTimeout`, `jQuery.html()`) without sanitization. Unlike reflected/stored XSS, payloads never reach the server. Detect by auditing JavaScript for tainted data flow from DOM sources to sinks. Tools: Burp Suite DOM Invader, Chrome DevTools, DOMPurify (fix). ([ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge](https://github.com/ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge/tree/HEAD/skills/curated/dom-xss) · ★27 · 7 copies)
- **dom-xss**: Use when XSS depends on browser-side sources and sinks such as URL/query/hash, router state, local/session storage, cookies, postMessage, DOM parsing, framework render paths, or client-side sanitizer behavior. ([ghostonbutterbread/bug-bounty-harness](https://github.com/ghostonbutterbread/bug-bounty-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/dom-xss))
- **dom-xss-analysis**: Analyze web applications for DOM-based Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) vulnerabilities. Use this skill whenever the user needs to find, understand, or exploit DOM XSS issues in JavaScript code, HTML pages, or web applications. Trigger on requests about DOM vulnerabilities, JavaScript injection, unsafe sinks, source-to-sink data flow, or any XSS-related security testing. ([Cstrien/Skills_Dior](https://github.com/Cstrien/Skills_Dior/tree/HEAD/pentesting-web/xss-cross-site-scripting/dom-xss) · ★1)
- **dom-xss-innerhtml-refactorer**: Audits, detects, and refactors DOM-based Cross-Site Scripting (XSS) vulnerabilities caused by unsafe dynamic innerHTML assignments across TypeScript/JavaScript codebases into safe DOM manipulation APIs with modernized unit test fixtures. ([bjohnson1279/skills-hub](https://github.com/bjohnson1279/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/dom-xss-innerhtml-refactorer))
